TOOLKIT & METHODOLOGY\n\n## 3.1 TOOL SELECTION PRINCIPLES\n\n- IMAGE TOOL PRIORITY:\n  * For any request involving image creation, modification, style change, or visual design, ALWAYS prefer the Image Tool.\n  * Do NOT describe images purely in text when an image output is expected.\n  * If an image result is required, the Image Tool is mandatory.\n\n- CLI TOOLS PREFERENCE:\n  * Always prefer CLI tools over Python scripts when possible\n  * CLI tools are generally faster and more efficient for:\n    1. Synchronous Commands (blocking):\n      * You can omit `blocking`, as it defaults to true\n      * Use for quick operations that complete within 60 seconds\n      * Commands run directly and wait for completion\n      * Example:\n        <function_calls>\n        <execute-command session_name="default" blocking="true">\n        ls -l\n        </execute-command>\n        </function_calls>\n        (or simply omit the blocking parameter as it defaults to true)\n      * IMPORTANT: Do not use for long-running operations as they will timeout after 60 seconds\n\n  2. Asynchronous Commands (non-blocking):\n      * Use `blocking="false"` for any command that might take longer than 60 seconds or for starting background services\n      * Commands run in background and return immediately\n      * Example:\n        <function_calls>\n        <execute-command session_name="dev" blocking="false">\n        npm run dev\n        </execute-command>\n        </function_calls>\n      * Common use cases:\n        - Development servers (Next.js, React, etc.)\n        - Build processes\n        - Long-running data processing\n        - Background services\n\n- Session Management:\n  * Each command must specify a session_name\n  * Use consistent session names for related commands\n  * Different sessions are isolated from each other\n  * Example: Use "build" session for build commands, "dev" for development servers\n  * Sessions maintain state between commands\n\n- Command Execution Guidelines:\n  * For commands that might take longer than 60 seconds, ALWAYS use run_async="true"\n  * Do not rely on increasing timeout for long-running commands\n  * Use proper session names for organization\n  * Chain commands with && for sequential execution\n  * Use | for piping output between commands\n  * Redirect output to files for long-running processes\n\n- Avoid commands requiring confirmation; actively use -y or -f flags for automatic confirmation\n- Avoid commands with excessive output; save to files when necessary\n- Chain multiple commands with operators to minimize interruptions and improve efficiency:\n  1. Use && for sequential execution: `command1 && command2 && command3`\n  2. Use || for fallback execution: `command1 || command2`\n  3. Use ; for unconditional execution: `command1; command2`\n  4. Use | for piping output: `command1 | command2`\n  5. Use > and >> for output redirection: `command > file` or `command >> file`\n- Use pipe operator to pass command outputs, simplifying operations\n- Use non-interactive `bc` for simple calculations, Python for complex math; never calculate mentally\n- Use `uptime` command when users explicitly request sandbox status check or wake-up\n\n## 3.3 CODE DEVELOPMENT PRACTICES\n- CODING:\n  * Must save code to files before execution; direct code input to interpreter commands is forbidden\n  * Write Python code for complex mathematical calculations and analysis\n  * Use search tools to find solutions when encountering unfamiliar problems\n  * For index.html, use deployment tools directly, or package everything into a zip file and provide it as a message attachment\n  * When creating web interfaces, always create CSS files first before HTML to ensure proper styling and design consistency\n  * For images, use real image URLs from sources like unsplash.com, pexels.com, pixabay.com, giphy.com, or wikimedia.org instead of creating placeholder images; use placeholder.com only as a last resort\n\n- WEBSITE DEPLOYMENT:\n  * Only use the \'deploy\' tool when users explicitly request permanent deployment to a production environment\n  * The deploy tool publishes static HTML+CSS+JS sites to a public URL using S3 web Pages\n  * If the same name is used for deployment, it will redeploy to the same project as before\n  * For temporary or development purposes, serve files locally instead of using the deployment tool\n  * When editing HTML files, always share the preview URL provided by the automatically running HTTP server with the user\n  * The preview URL is automatically generated and available in the tool results when creating or editing HTML files\n  * When deploying, ensure all assets (images, scripts, stylesheets) use relative paths to work correctly\n\n- PYTHON EXECUTION: Create reusable modules with proper error handling and logging. Use -nopgbrk to remove page breaks\n  2. pdfinfo: Get PDF metadata\n    - Use to check PDF properties\n    - Extract page count and dimensions\n  3. pdfimages: Extract images from PDFs\n    - Use -j to convert to JPEG\n    - Use for text replacement\n    - Use for pattern matching\n- File Analysis:\n  1. file: Determine file type\n  2. wc: Count words/lines\n  3. head/tail: View file parts\n  4. less: View large files\n- Data Processing:\n  1. jq: JSON processing\n    - Use -n to specify number of lines\n    - Use -f to follow file changes\n  3. awk: Pattern scanning and processing\n    - Use for column-based data processing\n    - Use for complex text transformations\n  4. find: Locate files and directories\n    - Use for precise text matching\n  2. Combine with CLI tools for powerful searches\n  3. Save complex patterns to files for reuse\n  4. Test patterns with small samples first\n  5. Each section contains specific, actionable subtasks based on complexity - use only as many as needed, no more\n3. Each task should be specific, actionable, and have clear completion criteria\n4. MUST actively work through these tasks one by one, checking them off as completed\n5. Adapt the plan as needed while maintaining its integrity as your execution compass\n\n## 5.2 TODO.MD FILE STRUCTURE AND USAGE\nThe todo.md file is your primary working document and action plan:\n\n1. Contains the complete list of tasks you MUST complete to fulfill the user\'s request\n2. Format with clear sections, each containing specific tasks marked with [ ] (incomplete) or [x] (complete)\n3. Each task should be specific, actionable, and have clear completion criteria\n4. MUST actively work through these tasks one by one, checking them off as completed\n5. Before every action, consult your todo.md to determine which task to tackle next\n6. The todo.md serves as your instruction set - if a task is in todo.md, you are responsible for completing it\n7. Update the todo.md as you make progress, adding new tasks as needed and marking completed ones\n8. Never delete tasks from todo.md - instead mark them complete with [x] to maintain a record of your work\n9. Once ALL tasks in todo.md are marked complete [x], you MUST call either the \'complete\' state or \'ask\' tool to signal task completion\n10. SCOPE CONSTRAINT: Focus on completing existing tasks before adding new ones; avoid continuously expanding scope\n11. CAPABILITY AWARENESS: Only add tasks that are achievable with your available tools and capabilities\n12. FINALITY: After marking a section complete, do not reopen it or add new tasks unless explicitly directed by the user\n13. STOPPING CONDITION: If you\'ve made 3 consecutive updates to todo.md without completing any tasks, reassess your approach and either simplify your plan or **use the \'ask\' tool to seek user guidance.**\n14. COMPLETION VERIFICATION: Only mark a task as [x] complete when you have concrete evidence of completion\n15. SIMPLICITY: Keep your todo.md lean and direct with clear actions, avoiding unnecessary verbosity or granularity\n\n## 5.3 EXECUTION PHILOSOPHY\nYour approach is deliberately methodical and persistent:\n\n1. Operate in a continuous loop until explicitly stopped\n2. Execute one step at a time, following a consistent loop: evaluate state → select tool → execute → provide narrative update → track progress\n3. Every action is guided by your todo.md, consulting it before selecting any tool\n4. Thoroughly verify each completed step before moving forward\n5. **Provide Markdown-formatted narrative updates directly in your responses** to keep the user informed of your progress, explain your thinking, and clarify the next steps. Use headers, brief descriptions, and context to make your process transparent.\n6. CRITICALLY IMPORTANT: Continue running in a loop until either:\n  - Using the **\'ask\' tool (THE ONLY TOOL THE USER CAN RESPOND TO)** to wait for essential user input (this pauses the loop)\n  - Using the \'complete\' tool when ALL tasks are finished\n7. For casual conversation:\n  - Use **\'ask\'** to properly end the conversation and wait for user input (**USER CAN RESPOND**)\n8. For tasks:\n  - Use **\'ask\'** when you need essential user input to proceed (**USER CAN RESPOND**)\n  - Provide **narrative updates** frequently in your responses to keep the user informed without requiring their input\n  - Use \'complete\' only when ALL tasks are finished\n9. MANDATORY COMPLETION:\n  - IMMEDIATELY use \'complete\' or \'ask\' after ALL tasks in todo.md are marked [x]\n  - NO additional commands or verifications after all tasks are complete\n  - NO further exploration or information gathering after completion\n  - NO redundant checks or validations after completion\n  - FAILURE to use \'complete\' or \'ask\' after task completion is a critical error\n\n## 5.4 TASK MANAGEMENT CYCLE\n1. STATE EVALUATION: Examine Todo.md for priorities, analyze recent Tool Results for environment understanding, and review past actions for context\n2. TOOL SELECTION: Choose exactly one tool that advances the current todo item\n3. EXECUTION: Wait for tool execution and observe results\n4. **NARRATIVE UPDATE:** Provide a **Markdown-formatted** narrative update directly in your response before the next tool call. Include explanations of what you\'ve done, what you\'re about to do, and why. Use headers, brief paragraphs, and formatting to enhance readability.\n5. PROGRESS TRACKING: Update todo.md with completed items and new tasks\n6. METHODICAL ITERATION: Repeat until section completion\n7. SECTION TRANSITION: Document completion and move to next section\n8. COMPLETION: IMMEDIATELY use \'complete\' or \'ask\' when ALL tasks are finished\n\n# 6. CONTENT CREATION\n\n## 6.1 WRITING GUIDELINES\n- Write content in continuous paragraphs using varied sentence lengths for engaging prose; avoid list formatting\n- Use prose and paragraphs by default; Updated Mirror Engine (mirror_engine.py)\r\nIncludes validation, cleanup, and better error handling.\r\n\r\npython\r\nimport subprocess\r\nimport os\r\nfrom utils import sanitize_git_url, validate_github_url, cleanup_workspace\r\n\r\nWORKSPACE_DIR = "./mirror_workspace"\r\n\r\ndef run_mirror_sync(repo, gh_token, backup_token, cleanup_after=False):\r\n    source = sanitize_git_url(repo["source"])\r\n    destination = sanitize_git_url(repo["destination"])\r\n    repo_name = repo["name"]\r\n\r\n    # Validate before running\r\n    if not validate_github_url(source):\r\n        return False, f"Invalid source URL: {source}"\r\n    if not source.startswith("https://"):\r\n        return False, "Only HTTPS URLs are supported"\r\n\r\n    # Inject tokens\r\n    source_auth = source.replace("https://", f"https://oauth2:{gh_token}@")\r\n    dest_auth = destination.replace("https://", f"https://oauth2:{backup_token}@")\r\n\r\n    repo_dir = os.path.join(WORKSPACE_DIR, f"{repo_name}.git")\r\n    os.makedirs(WORKSPACE_DIR, exist_ok=True)\r\n\r\n    try:\r\n        if not os.path.exists(repo_dir):\r\n            subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "--mirror", source_auth, repo_dir], check=True, capture_output=True)\r\n        else:\r\n            subprocess.run(["git", "--git-dir", repo_dir, "remote", "update"], check=True, capture_output=True)\r\n\r\n        subprocess.run(["git", "--git-dir", repo_dir, "push", "--mirror", dest_auth], check=True, capture_output=True)\r\n\r\n        if cleanup_after:\r\n            cleanup_workspace(WORKSPACE_DIR, repo_name)\r\n\r\n        return True, f"Sync completed for {repo_name}"\r\n\r\n    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:\r\n        error_msg = e.stderr.decode()\r\n        error_msg = error_msg.replace(gh_token, "***").replace(backup_token, "***")\r\n        return False, f"Git error: {error_msg}"\r\n📦 4. .gitignore\r\ngitignore\r\nconfig.json\r\n.env\r\nmirror_workspace/\r\n*.pyc\r\n__pycache__/\r\n*.log\r\n*.pid\r\n🐳 5. dependencies\r\n"""\r\n\r\nimport subprocess\r\nimport os\r\nfrom pathlib import Path\r\nfrom feddup.utils import sanitize_git_url, validate_source_url, cleanup_workspace\r\nfrom feddup.logger import get_logger\r\n\r\nlogger = get_logger(__name__)\r\nWORKSPACE_DIR = Path("./feddup_workspace")\r\n\r\ndef duplicate_repository(repo, gh_token, backup_token, cleanup=False):\r\n    """\r\n    The main duplication engine.\r\n    Clones --mirror from source, pushes --mirror to destination.\r\n    """\r\n    source = sanitize_git_url(repo["source"])\r\n    destination = sanitize_git_url(repo["destination"])\r\n    repo_name = repo["name"]\r\n\r\n    logger.info(f"🔄 Starting Fed-Dup for: {repo_name}")\r\n\r\n    # Validate\r\n    if not validate_source_url(source):\r\n        return False, f"Invalid source URL: {source}"\r\n\r\n    # Inject tokens\r\n    source_auth = source.replace("https://", f"https://oauth2:{gh_token}@")\r\n    dest_auth = destination.replace("https://", f"https://oauth2:{backup_token}@")\r\n\r\n    # Create workspace\r\n    WORKSPACE_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\r\n    repo_dir = WORKSPACE_DIR / f"{repo_name}.git"\r\n\r\n    try:\r\n        # Phase 1: Clone mirror\r\n        if not repo_dir.exists():\r\n            logger.info(f"📥 Fresh mirror clone of {repo_name}")\r\n            subprocess.run(\r\n                ["git", "clone", "--mirror", source_auth, str(repo_dir)],\r\n                check=True,\r\n                capture_output=True\r\n            )\r\n        else:\r\n            logger.info(f"🔄 Updating existing mirror of {repo_name}")\r\n            subprocess.run(\r\n                ["git", "--git-dir", str(repo_dir), "remote", "update"],\r\n                check=True,\r\n                capture_output=True\r\n            )\r\n\r\n        # Phase 2: Push mirror to destination\r\n        logger.info(f"📤 Pushing {repo_name} to backup destination")\r\n        subprocess.run(\r\n            ["git", "--git-dir", str(repo_dir), "push", "--mirror", dest_auth],\r\n            check=True,\r\n            capture_output=True\r\n        )\r\n\r\n        # Phase 3: Cleanup if requested\r\n        if cleanup:\r\n            cleanup_workspace(WORKSPACE_DIR, repo_name)\r\n            logger.info(f"🧹 Cleaned up workspace for {repo_name}")\r\n\r\n        logger.info(f"✅ Fed-Dup completed: {repo_name}")\r\n        return True, f"Fed-Dup successful for {repo_name}"\r\n\r\n    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:\r\n        error_msg = e.stderr.decode()\r\n        error_msg = error_msg.replace(gh_token, "***").replace(backup_token, "***")\r\n        logger.error(f"❌ Fed-Dup failed for {repo_name}: {error_msg}")\r\n        return False, f"Git error: {error_msg}"\r\n2. feddup/utils.py - Security & Cleanup\r\npython\r\n"""\r\nFed-Dup Utilities: Validation, sanitization, disk management\r\n"""\r\n\r\nimport re\r\nimport shutil\r\nfrom pathlib import Path\r\n\r\ndef sanitize_git_url(url: str) -> str:\r\n    """Clean URL: remove auth, ensure .git suffix"""\r\n    url = re.sub(r\'.*@\', \'\', url)\r\n    url = re.sub(r\'https?://[^:]+:[^@]+@\', \'https://\', url)\r\n    if not url.endswith(\'.git\'):\r\n        url += \'.git\'\r\n    return url\r\n\r\ndef validate_source_url(url: str) -> bool:\r\n    """Only allow HTTPS GitHub/GitLab sources"""\r\n    return (url.startswith("https://github.com/") or \r\n            url.startswith("https://gitlab.com/") or\r\n            url.startswith("https://bitbucket.org/"))\r\n\r\ndef validate_destination_url(url: str) -> bool:\r\n    """Validate backup destination"""\r\n    return url.startswith("https://")\r\n\r\ndef cleanup_workspace(workspace_dir: Path, repo_name: str) -> bool:\r\n    """Remove a repo\'s mirror cache to save disk space"""\r\n    repo_path = workspace_dir / f"{repo_name}.git"\r\n    if repo_path.exists():\r\n        shutil.rmtree(repo_path)\r\n        return True\r\n    return False\r\n\r\ndef get_repo_size(workspace_dir: Path, repo_name: str) -> int:\r\n    """Get size of cached repo in bytes"""\r\n    repo_path = workspace_dir / f"{repo_name}.git"\r\n    if repo_path.exists():\r\n        return sum(f.stat().st_size for f in repo_path.rglob(\'*\'))\r\n    return 0\r\n3. feddup/logger.py - Consistent Logging\r\npython\r\n"""\r\nFed-Dup Logging Configuration\r\n"""\r\n\r\nimport logging\r\nimport sys\r\n\r\ndef get_logger(name):\r\n    logger = logging.getLogger(name)\r\n    logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)\r\n    \r\n    handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)\r\n    handler.setFormatter(logging.Formatter(\r\n        \'%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s\',\r\n        datefmt=\'%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\'\r\n    ))\r\n    \r\n    if not logger.handlers:\r\n        logger.addHandler(handler)\r\n    \r\n    return logger\r\n4. feddup/config.py - Configuration Management\r\npython\r\n"""\r\nFed-Dup Configuration Handler (JSON-based, No Database)\r\n"""\r\n\r\nimport json\r\nfrom pathlib import Path\r\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Optional\r\n\r\nCONFIG_FILE = Path("config.json")\r\n\r\ndef load_config() -> Dict:\r\n    """Load config from JSON file"""\r\n    if not CONFIG_FILE.exists():\r\n        return {\r\n            "github_token": "",\r\n            "backup_token": "",\r\n            "repositories": [],\r\n            "settings": {\r\n                "auto_sync_interval": 3600,  # seconds\r\n                "cleanup_after_sync": False,\r\n                "max_parallel_syncs": 3\r\n            }\r\n        }\r\n    with open(CONFIG_FILE, "r") as f:\r\n        return json.load(f)\r\n\r\ndef save_config(config: Dict) -> None:\r\n    """Save config to JSON file"""\r\n    with open(CONFIG_FILE, "w") as f:\r\n        json.dump(config, f, indent=4)\r\n\r\ndef add_repository(config: Dict, name: str, source: str, destination: str) -> None:\r\n    """Add a repository to the config"""\r\n    config["repositories"].append({\r\n        "name": name,\r\n        "source": source,\r\n        "destination": destination\r\n    })\r\n    save_config(config)\r\n\r\ndef remove_repository(config: Dict, index: int) -> None:\r\n    """Remove a repository by index"""\r\n    config["repositories"].pop(index)\r\n    save_config(config)\r\n\r\ndef get_repository_count(config: Dict) -> int:\r\n    """Get number of configured repositories"""\r\n    return len(config.get("repositories", []))\r\n🎨 Updated app.py (Streamlit UI with Fed-Dup branding)\r\npython\r\n"""\r\nFed-Dup - Federated Repository Duplication\r\nWeb UI built with Streamlit\r\n"""\r\n\r\nimport streamlit as st\r\nimport sys\r\nfrom pathlib import Path\r\n\r\n# Add feddup module to path\r\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))\r\n\r\nfrom feddup.config import load_config, save_config, add_repository, remove_repository\r\nfrom feddup.engine import duplicate_repository\r\nfrom feddup.utils import get_repo_size\r\n\r\n# Page config\r\nst.set_page_config(\r\n    page_title="Fed-Dup",\r\n    page_icon="🛡️",\r\n    layout="wide"\r\n)\r\n\r\n# Custom CSS\r\nst.markdown("""\r\n<style>\r\n    .main-header {\r\n        font-size: 3rem;\r\n        font-weight: 700;\r\n        background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);\r\n        -webkit-background-clip: text;\r\n        -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;\r\n        padding: 1rem 0;\r\n    }\r\n    .status-success {\r\n        color: #00cc88;\r\n        font-weight: 600;\r\n    }\r\n    .status-error {\r\n        color: #ff6b6b;\r\n        font-weight: 600;\r\n    }\r\n</style>\r\n""", unsafe_allow_html=True)\r\n\r\n# Header\r\nst.markdown(\'<div class="main-header">🛡️ Fed-Dup</div>\', unsafe_allow_html=True)\r\nst.caption("*Federated Repository Duplication Engine*")\r\nst.divider()\r\n\r\n# Load config\r\nconfig = load_config()\r\n\r\n# Sidebar\r\nwith st.sidebar:\r\n    st.subheader("⚙️ Fed-Dup Settings")\r\n    \r\n    # Credentials\r\n    with st.expander("🔑 Credentials", expanded=True):\r\n        gh_token = st.text_input(\r\n            "GitHub Token", \r\n            value=config.get("github_token", ""),\r\n            type="password",\r\n            help="Personal Access Token with repo access"\r\n        )\r\n        backup_token = st.text_input(\r\n            "Backup Platform Token", \r\n            value=config.get("backup_token", ""),\r\n            type="password",\r\n            help="Token for your backup Git host"\r\n        )\r\n        \r\n        if st.button("💾 Save Credentials", use_container_width=True):\r\n            config["github_token"] = gh_token\r\n            config["backup_token"] = backup_token\r\n            save_config(config)\r\n            st.success("✅ Credentials saved securely")\r\n    \r\n    # Settings\r\n    with st.expander("⚡ Sync Settings"):\r\n        cleanup = st.toggle(\r\n            "🧹 Cleanup after sync",\r\n            value=config.get("settings", {}).get("cleanup_after_sync", False),\r\n            help="Delete local mirror cache after push to save disk space"\r\n        )\r\n        config["settings"] = config.get("settings", {})\r\n        config["settings"]["cleanup_after_sync"] = cleanup\r\n        save_config(config)\r\n        \r\n        st.caption(f"📁 Workspace: `./feddup_workspace/`")\r\n\r\n# Main content - Two columns\r\ncol1, col2 = st.columns([2, 1])\r\n\r\nwith col1:\r\n    st.subheader("📦 Repositories to Duplicate")\r\n    \r\n    # Add repo form\r\n    with st.expander("➕ Add Repository", expanded=False):\r\n        with st.form("add_repo", clear_on_submit=True):\r\n            name = st.text_input("Friendly Name", placeholder="my-awesome-project")\r\n            source = st.text_input("Source URL", placeholder="https://github.com/user/repo.git")\r\n            destination = st.text_input("Destination URL", placeholder="https://gitlab.com/user/repo.git")\r\n            \r\n            if st.form_submit_button("Add to Fed-Dup", use_container_width=True):\r\n                if name and source and destination:\r\n                    add_repository(config, name, source, destination)\r\n                    st.success(f"✅ Added {name} to duplication queue")\r\n                    st.rerun()\r\n                else:\r\n                    st.error("❌ All fields are required")\r\n    \r\n    # Repository list\r\n    repos = config.get("repositories", [])\r\n    \r\n    if not repos:\r\n        st.info("💡 No repositories configured. Add one above to start duplicating.")\r\n    else:\r\n        for idx, repo in enumerate(repos):\r\n            with st.container():\r\n                cols = st.columns([3, 1.5, 0.8, 0.8])\r\n                \r\n                with cols[0]:\r\n                    st.markdown(f"**{repo[\'name\']}**")\r\n                    st.caption(f"📍 {repo[\'source\']}")\r\n                    st.caption(f"🎯 {repo[\'destination\']}")\r\n                \r\n                with cols[1]:\r\n                    # Check workspace size\r\n                    workspace_path = Path("./feddup_workspace")\r\n                    size = get_repo_size(workspace_path, repo[\'name\'])\r\n                    if size > 0:\r\n                        size_mb = size / (1024 * 1024)\r\n                        st.caption(f"💾 {size_mb:.1f} MB cached")\r\n                \r\n                with cols[2]:\r\n                    if st.button("🔄 Sync", key=f"sync_{idx}"):\r\n                        if not gh_token or not backup_token:\r\n                            st.error("❌ Please configure credentials first")\r\n                        else:\r\n                            with st.spinner(f"⏳ Duplicating {repo[\'name\']}..."):\r\n                                success, msg = duplicate_repository(\r\n                                    repo, \r\n                                    gh_token, \r\n                                    backup_token,\r\n                                    cleanup=config.get("settings", {}).get("cleanup_after_sync", False)\r\n                                )\r\n                                if success:\r\n                                    st.success(f"✅ {msg}")\r\n                                else:\r\n                                    st.error(f"❌ {msg}")\r\n                \r\n                with cols[3]:\r\n                    if st.button("🗑️", key=f"del_{idx}", help="Remove from Fed-Dup"):\r\n                        remove_repository(config, idx)\r\n                        st.rerun()\r\n                \r\n                st.divider()\r\n\r\nwith col2:\r\n    st.subheader("📊 Dashboard")\r\n    \r\n    # Stats\r\n    st.metric("📦 Repositories", len(repos))\r\n    st.metric("🔄 Auto-Sync Interval", f"{config.get(\'settings\', {}).get(\'auto_sync_interval\', 3600) // 60} min")\r\n    st.metric("🧹 Cleanup", "Enabled" if config.get("settings", {}).get("cleanup_after_sync", False) else "Disabled")\r\n    \r\n    # Quick sync all\r\n    if repos:\r\n        if st.button("🔄 Sync All Now", use_container_width=True, type="primary"):\r\n            if not gh_token or not backup_token:\r\n                st.error("❌ Please configure credentials first")\r\n            else:\r\n                progress_bar = st.progress(0)\r\n                status_text = st.empty()\r\n                \r\n                for i, repo in enumerate(repos):\r\n                    status_text.text(f"⏳ Syncing: {repo[\'name\']}...")\r\n                    success, msg = duplicate_repository(\r\n                        repo, \r\n                        gh_token, \r\n                        backup_token,\r\n                        cleanup=config.get("settings", {}).get("cleanup_after_sync", False)\r\n                    )\r\n                    progress_bar.progress((i + 1) / len(repos))\r\n                \r\n                status_text.text("✅ All repositories synced!")\r\n    \r\n    st.divider()\r\n    \r\n    # Quick help\r\n    with st.expander("❓ How to use Fed-Dup"):\r\n        st.markdown("""\r\n        1. **Generate a GitHub PAT** with `repo` scope\r\n        2. **Generate a token** for your backup platform\r\n        3. **Add repositories** you want to duplicate\r\n        4. **Click Sync** to duplicate immediately\r\n        5. **Fed-Dup auto-syncs** every hour in the background\r\n        """)\r\n\r\n# Footer\r\nst.divider()\r\nst.caption("🛡️ Fed-Dup v1.0.0 • Federated Repository Duplication Engine • No database required")\r\n🐳 Updated Dockerfile\r\ndockerfile\r\nFROM python:3.11-slim\r\n\r\nLABEL maintainer="Fed-Dup Team"\r\nLABEL description="Federated Repository Duplication Engine"\r\n\r\n# Install Git\r\nRUN apt-get update && \\\r\n    apt-get install -y git && \\\r\n    rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*\r\n\r\nWORKDIR /app\r\n\r\n# Copy requirements first for better caching\r\nCOPY requirements.txt .\r\nRUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt\r\n\r\n# Copy the entire application\r\nCOPY . .\r\n\r\n# Create workspace directory\r\nRUN mkdir -p /app/feddup_workspace\r\n\r\n# Expose Streamlit port\r\nEXPOSE 8501\r\n\r\n# Health check\r\nHEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=10s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \\\r\n    CMD python -c "import requests; requests.get(\'http://localhost:8501\')" || exit 1\r\n\r\n# Run both UI and worker\r\nCMD streamlit run app.py --server.port=8501 --server.address=0.0.0.0 & \\\r\n    python -c "from worker import auto_sync_loop; auto_sync_loop()"\r\n📦 requirements.txt\r\ntext\r\nstreamlit>=1.28.0\r\n📋 .gitignore\r\ngitignore\r\n# Fed-Dup Engine*\r\n\r\n[![License: MIT](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-blue.svg)](LICENSE)\r\n[![Python 3.11+](https://img.shields.io/badge/python-3.11+-blue.svg)](https://www.python.org/)\r\n[![Streamlit](https://img.shields.io/badge/Streamlit-1.28-red.svg)](https://streamlit.io/)\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 🎯 What is Fed-Dup?\r\n\r\n**Fed-Dup** is a lightweight, dependency-free tool that **duplicates your Git repositories** to a secondary platform. Think of it as an insurance policy for your code.\r\n\r\nIf GitHub ever goes down, your repositories are **already mirrored** on GitLab, Codeberg, or any Git server you choose.\r\n\r\n### ✨ Key Features\r\n\r\n- 🔄 **Full mirror duplication** - clones ALL branches, tags, and commit history\r\n- 🎨 **Beautiful Web UI** - manage everything through Streamlit\r\n- 🤖 **Automatic background sync** - runs hourly, no manual intervention needed\r\n- 🧹 **Disk space management** - delete local cache after sync if you want\r\n- 🐳 **Docker-ready** - deploy in one command\r\n- 🔒 **Secure** - tokens stored locally, never logged\r\n- 💾 **No database** - everything stored in a single JSON file\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 🚀 Quick Start\r\n\r\n### Option 1: Docker (Recommended)\r\n\r\n```bash\r\ngit clone https://github.com/yourname/fed-dup\r\ncd fed-dup\r\ncp config.json.example config.json\r\n# Edit config.json with your tokens and repos\r\ndocker-compose up -d\r\nAccess the UI at http://localhost:8501\r\n\r\nOption 2: Local Installation\r\nbash\r\ngit clone https://github.com/yourname/fed-dup\r\ncd fed-dup\r\npip install -r requirements.txt\r\ncp config.json.example config.json\r\n# Edit config.json with your tokens and repos\r\nstreamlit run app.py\r\n🔑 Setting Up Tokens\r\nGitHub Personal Access Token\r\nGo to GitHub Settings → Developer Settings → Personal Access Tokens\r\n\r\nGenerate a token with repo scope\r\n\r\nCopy the token\r\n\r\nBackup Platform Token\r\nGitLab: Settings → Access Tokens → api scope\r\n\r\nGitea: Settings → Applications → Generate token\r\n\r\nOther: Use password or SSH (SSH not yet supported)\r\n\r\n📁 Configuration\r\nFed-Dup uses a single config.json file:\r\n\r\njson\r\n{\r\n  "github_token": "ghp_xxxxx",\r\n  "backup_token": "glpat_xxxxx",\r\n  "settings": {\r\n    "auto_sync_interval": 3600,\r\n    "cleanup_after_sync": false,\r\n    "max_parallel_syncs": 3\r\n  },\r\n  "repositories": [\r\n    {\r\n      "name": "my-project",\r\n      "source": "https://github.com/username/my-project.git",\r\n      "destination": "https://gitlab.com/username/my-project.git"\r\n    }\r\n  ]\r\n}\r\n🏗️ Architecture\r\ntext\r\n┌─────────────┐      ┌──────────────┐      ┌─────────────────┐\r\n│  GitHub     │ ──▶ │  Fed-Dup     │ ──▶ │  Backup Host   │\r\n│  (Source)   │      │  Engine      │      │  (GitLab/etc)  │\r\n└─────────────┘      └──────────────┘      └─────────────────┘\r\n                            │\r\n                            ▼\r\n                     ┌──────────────┐\r\n                     │  Web UI      │\r\n                     │  (Streamlit) │\r\n                     └──────────────┘\r\n🛠️ Development\r\nFile Structure\r\ntext\r\nfed-dup/\r\n├── app.py              # Streamlit UI\r\n├── worker.py           # Auto-sync background worker\r\n├── feddup/\r\n│   ├── __init__.py\r\n│   ├── engine.py       # Core duplication logic\r\n│   ├── config.py       # Config management\r\n│   ├── utils.py        # Validation & cleanup\r\n│   └── logger.py       # Logging setup\r\n├── Dockerfile\r\n├── docker-compose.yml\r\n└── config.json.example\r\nRunning Tests\r\nbash\r\npython -m unittest discover tests/\r\n🔐 Security Notes\r\nTokens are stored only in config.json on your local machine/server\r\n\r\nTokens are never displayed in logs or UI\r\n\r\nAlways use .gitignore to prevent committing config.json\r\n\r\nUse read-only tokens when possible\r\n\r\n🤝 Contributing\r\nFork the repo\r\n\r\nCreate a feature branch\r\n\r\nCommit your changes\r\n\r\nOpen a Pull Request\r\n\r\n📄 License\r\nMIT License - see LICENSE file\r\n\r\n💬 Support\r\nIssues: GitHub Issues\r\n\r\nDiscussions: GitHub Discussions\r\n\r\n⭐ Star History\r\nIf you actions/setup-python@v5\r\n      with:\r\n        python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}\r\n    \r\n    - name: Install dependencies\r\n      run: |\r\n        python -m pip install --upgrade pip\r\n        pip install -r requirements.txt\r\n        pip install flake8 pytest black mypy\r\n    \r\n    - name: Lint with flake8\r\n      run: |\r\n        flake8 feddup/ app.py worker.py --count --max-complexity=10 --statistics\r\n    \r\n    - name: Format with black\r\n      run: |\r\n        black --check feddup/ app.py worker.py\r\n    \r\n    - name: Type check with mypy\r\n      run: |\r\n        mypy feddup/ --ignore-missing-imports\r\n    \r\n    - name: Test with pytest\r\n      run: |\r\n        pytest tests/ -v --cov=feddup --cov-report=xml\r\n    \r\n    - name: Upload steps:\r\n    - uses: actions/checkout@v4\r\n    \r\n    - name: Set up Python\r\n      uses: actions/setup-python@v5\r\n      with:\r\n        python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}\r\n    \r\n    - name: Install dependencies\r\n      run: |\r\n        pip install -r requirements.txt\r\n        pip install -r dev-requirements.txt 2>/dev/null || true\r\n    \r\n    - name: Lint\r\n      run: |\r\n        pip install flake8 black mypy\r\n        flake8 feddup/ app.py worker.py\r\n        black --check feddup/ app.py worker.py\r\n        mypy feddup/ --ignore-missing-imports\r\n    \r\n    - name: Test\r\n      run: |\r\n        pip install pytest pytest-cov\r\n        pytest tests/ -v --cov=feddup --cov-report=xml\r\n    \r\n    - name: Security check\r\n      run: |\r\n        pip install bandit\r\n        bandit -r feddup/ -f json -o bandit-report.json || true\r\n    \r\n    - name: Upload coverage\r\n      uses: codecov/codecov-action@v4\r\n      with:\r\n        file: ./coverage.xml\r\n        flags: unittests\r\n        name: feddup-coverage\r\n9. .github/workflows/cd.yml\r\nyaml\r\nname: CD\r\n\r\non:\r\n  push:\r\n    tags:\r\n      - \'v*\'\r\n\r\njobs:\r\n }}"\r\n        if [[ ! "$PR_TITLE" =~ ^(feat|fix|docs|style|refactor|test|chore|perf|ci|build|revert)(\\(.+\\))?: ]]; then\r\n          echo "❌ PR title must follow Conventional Commits format"\r\n          echo "Expected: <type>(<scope>): <description>"\r\n          echo "Got: $PR_TITLE"\r\n          exit 1\r\n        fi\r\n    \r\n    - name: Validate PR description\r\n      run: |\r\n        if [ -z "${{ github.event.pull_request.body }}" ]; then\r\n          echo "⚠️ PR description is empty"\r\n          exit 1\r\n        fi\r\n14. .github/workflows/stale.yml\r\nyaml\r\nname: Stale\r\n\r\non:\r\n  schedule:\r\n    - cron: \'0 0 * * *\'  # Daily\r\n  workflow_dispatch:\r\n\r\njobs:\r\n  stale:\r\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\r\n    permissions:\r\n      issues: write\r\n      pull-requests: write\r\n    \r\n    steps:\r\n    - uses: actions/stale@v9\r\n      with:\r\n        stale-issue-message: |\r\n          This issue has been automatically marked as stale because it has not had recent activity.\r\n          It will be closed if no further activity occurs.\r\n          Thank you for your contributions to Fed-Dup! name: Deploy to GitHub Pages\r\n      id: deployment\r\n      uses: actions/deploy-pages@v4\r\n20. .github/workflows/dependency-review.yml\r\nyaml\r\nname: Dependency Review\r\n\r\non:\r\n  pull_request:\r\n    branches: [ main ]\r\n\r\npermissions:\r\n  contents: read\r\n\r\njobs:\r\n  dependency-review:\r\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\r\n    \r\n    steps:\r\n    - name: Checkout\r\n      uses: actions/checkout@v4\r\n    \r\n    - name: Dependency Review\r\n      uses: actions/dependency-review-action@v4\r\n      with:\r\n        fail-on-severity: high\r\n        fail-on-scopes: runtime\r\n        comment-summary-in-pr: true\r\n21. .github/workflows/scorecards.yml\r\nyaml\r\nname: Scorecards\r\n\r\non:\r\n  branch_protection_rule:\r\n  schedule:\r\n    - cron: \'0 0 * * *\'  # Daily\r\n  push:\r\n    branches: [ main ]\r\n  workflow_dispatch:\r\n\r\npermissions: read-all\r\n\r\njobs:\r\n  analysis:\r\n    name: Scorecards analysis\r\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\r\n    permissions:\r\n      security-events: write\r\n      id-token: write\r\n    \r\n    steps:\r\n    - name: Checkout\r\n      uses: actions/checkout@v4\r\n      with:\r\n        persist-credentials: false\r\n    \r\n    - name: Run analysis\r\n      uses: ossf/scorecard-action@v2\r\n      with:\r\n        results_file: results.sarif\r\n        results_format: sarif\r\n        repo_token: ${{ secrets.SCORECARD_TOKEN }}\r\n        publish_results: true\r\n    \r\n    - name: Upload to code-scanning\r\n      uses: github/codeql-action/upload-sarif@v3\r\n      with:\r\n        sarif_file: results.sarif\r\n22. CLAUDE.md\r\nmarkdown\r\n# Claude Development Guidelines for Fed-Dup\r\n\r\n## 🎯 Project Purpose\r\nFed-Dup is a federated repository duplication engine - mirroring Git repositories to backup platforms.\r\n\r\n## 🏗️ Architecture\r\n- **UI Layer**: Streamlit web interface\r\n- **Core Engine**: Python with subprocess for Git operations\r\n- **Config**: JSON-based, no database\r\n- **Deployment**: Docker + Docker Compose\r\n\r\n## 📁 Code Structure\r\nfeddup/ # Core package\r\n├── engine.py # Mirror logic\r\n├── config.py # Config management\r\n├── utils.py # Helpers\r\n└── logger.py # Logging\r\ntests/ # Unit tests\r\napp.py # Streamlit UI\r\nworker.py # Background sync\r\n\r\ntext\r\n\r\n## 🛠️ Development Rules\r\n1. **No external dependencies beyond Streamlit**\r\n2. **Never log tokens or credentials**\r\n3. **Validate all URLs before Git operations**\r\n4. **Handle subprocess errors gracefully**\r\n5. **Write tests for all new features**\r\n\r\n## 🔐 Security Requirements\r\n- Tokens stored only in config.json\r\n- Never display tokens in UI/logs\r\n- Validate URLs before processing\r\n- Sanitize all user input\r\n\r\n## 🧪 Testing\r\n- Run: `pytest tests/ -v`\r\n- Coverage: `pytest --cov=feddup tests/`\r\n- Lint: `flake8 feddup/`\r\n- Format: `black feddup/`\r\n\r\n## 📝 Commit Conventions\r\n- `feat:` New feature\r\n- `fix:` Bug fix\r\n- `docs:` Documentation\r\n- `refactor:` Code refactoring\r\n- `test:` Test updates\r\n- `chore:` Maintenance\r\n\r\n## 🚀 Deployment\r\n- Docker: `docker-compose up -d`\r\n- Local: `streamlit run app.py`\r\n- Worker runs automatically in background\r\n23. AGENTS.md\r\nmarkdown\r\n# AI Agents & Automation Guide for Fed-Dup\r\n\r\n## 🤖 Agent Configuration\r\n\r\n### GitHub Actions\r\n- CI/CD pipelines in `.github/workflows/`\r\n- Automated testing, building, and deployment\r\n- Security scanning with CodeQL and Scorecards\r\n\r\n### Dependabot\r\nUpdate dependencies automatically:\r\n```yaml\r\nversion: 2\r\nupdates:\r\n  - package-ecosystem: "pip"\r\n    directory: "/"\r\n    schedule:\r\n      interval: "weekly"\r\n    open-pull-requests-limit: 10\r\nCode Review Bot\r\nAutomate PR reviews with GitHub Actions\r\n\r\nCheck conventional commits\r\n\r\nValidate PR structure\r\n\r\n🔄 Automation Workflows\r\nTrigger\tAction\r\nPush to main\tRun CI, build, test, deploy\r\nPR opened\tRun tests, lint, security scan\r\nPR merged\tDeploy to staging\r\nTag created\tRelease to PyPI and Docker Hub\r\nDaily\tRun comprehensive tests\r\n🛡️ Security Automation\r\nCodeQL scanning on every PR\r\n\r\nDependency vulnerability scanning\r\n\r\nSecrets detection in PRs\r\n\r\nWeekly security audits\r\n\r\n📊 Monitoring\r\nTest coverage reporting to Codecov\r\n\r\nPerformance metrics tracking\r\n\r\nError logging and alerting\r\n\r\n🔧 Agent Commands\r\nFor Testing\r\nbash\r\n# Run all tests\r\npytest tests/ -v\r\n\r\n# Run with coverage\r\npytest --cov=feddup tests/\r\n\r\n# Lint code\r\nflake8 feddup/\r\n\r\n# Format code\r\nblack feddup/\r\nFor Deployment\r\nbash\r\n# Build Docker image\r\ndocker build -t feddup .\r\n\r\n# Run locally\r\ndocker-compose up -d\r\n\r\n# Push to PyPI\r\npython -m build && twine upload dist/*\r\n🧠 AI-Assisted Development\r\nUse GitHub Copilot for code completion\r\n\r\nChatGPT for complex algorithm explanations\r\n\r\nClaude for architectural decisions\r\n\r\n📈 Continuous Improvement\r\nReview automated test results\r\n\r\nMonitor security alerts\r\n\r\nTrack deployment success rate\r\n\r\nAnalyze user feedback from issues/discussions\r\n\r\ntext\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n### 24. `AUTHORS.md`\r\n\r\n```markdown\r\n# Fed-Dup Authors\r\n\r\n## 🛡️ Core Team\r\n\r\n### Founder & Lead Developer\r\n- **Your Name** - [@yourusername](https://github.com/yourusername)\r\n  - Architecture & Core Engine\r\n  - Streamlit UI\r\n  - Documentation\r\n\r\n## 🎯 Contributors\r\n\r\n<!-- List contributors in alphabetical order -->\r\n\r\n## 📊 By the Numbers\r\n- **Total Contributors**: 1\r\n- **Lines of Code**: ~2000\r\n- **Core Modules**: 5\r\n- **Tests**: 15+\r\n\r\n## 💜 Thank You\r\nEvery contribution, whether code, documentation, or feedback, makes Fed-Dup better.\r\n\r\n### How to Add Yourself\r\n1. Fork the repository\r\n2. Add your name to this file in alphabetical order\r\n3. Submit a PR with your changes\r\n\r\n## 📝 Format\r\nYour Name - @github_handle\r\n\r\nWhat you contributed\r\n\r\nAny special acknowledgments\r\n\r\ntext\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n**Together we\'re building a safer open source ecosystem! 🛡️**\r\n25. MAINTAINERS.md\r\nmarkdown\r\n# Fed-Dup Maintainers Guide\r\n\r\n## 👤 Current Maintainers\r\n\r\n### Lead Maintainer\r\n- **Your Name** - [@yourusername](https://github.com/yourusername)\r\n  - Project direction\r\n  - Code reviews\r\n  - Release management\r\n\r\n## 🔧 Maintainer Responsibilities\r\n\r\n### Code Review\r\n- Review PRs within 48 hours\r\n- Ensure tests pass and code coverage doesn\'t decrease\r\n- Check for security issues\r\n- Verify documentation updates\r\n\r\n### Release Management\r\n- Create release tags (v1.0.0 format)\r\n- Write release notes\r\n- Publish to PyPI and Docker Hub\r\n- Announce releases\r\n\r\n### Community Management\r\n- Triage issues\r\n- Answer questions in discussions\r\n- Label and organize issues/PRs\r\n- Enforce Code of Conduct\r\n\r\n### Documentation\r\n- Keep README up to date\r\n- Maintain examples\r\n- Update CHANGELOG.md\r\n- Ensure API documentation is complete\r\n\r\n## 🚀 Release Process\r\n\r\n1. Ensure all tests pass\r\n2. Update CHANGELOG.md\r\n3. Bump version in `feddup/__init__.py`\r\n4. Create PR for version bump\r\n5. After merge, create tag `vX.Y.Z`\r\n6. GitHub Actions handles publishing\r\n\r\n## 🛡️ Security Response\r\n\r\n### Security Vulnerability Handling\r\n1. Create a private security advisory\r\n2. Investigate and fix issue\r\n3. Release patch version\r\n4. Announce fix in security advisory\r\n\r\n### Responsible Disclosure\r\n- Security issues should be reported via GitHub Security Advisory\r\n- Do not open public issues for security vulnerabilities\r\n\r\n## 📋 Merge Criteria\r\n\r\n- ✅ All CI checks pass\r\n- ✅ At least one maintainer approval\r\n- ✅ Test coverage doesn\'t decrease\r\n- ✅ Documentation updated\r\n- ✅ No breaking changes without discussion\r\n\r\n## 🔐 Access Control\r\n\r\n### GitHub Permissions\r\n- **Maintain**: Full repository access\r\n- **Triage**: Issue/PR management\r\n- **Write**: Push to branches, create releases\r\n\r\n### PyPI Ownership\r\n- Current maintainer is the only PyPI uploader\r\n- Add maintainers via PyPI project settings\r\n\r\n### Docker Hub Ownership\r\n- Fed-Dup image under organization namespace\r\n- Maintainers added as collaborators\r\n\r\n## 📊 Monitoring\r\n\r\nTrack these metrics weekly:\r\n- Active issues/PRs\r\n- Response time\r\n- Release adoption\r\n- Test coverage percentage\r\n\r\n## 💡 Best Practices\r\n\r\n1. **Be welcoming** to new contributors\r\n2. **Respond quickly** to questions\r\n3. **Document decisions** in ADR.md\r\n4. **Keep dependencies minimal**\r\n5. **Test before merging**\r\n6. **Write clear commit messages**\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n**Thank you for maintaining Fed-Dup! 🛡️**\r\n26. docs/ADR.md\r\nmarkdown\r\n# Architecture Decision Records\r\n\r\n## 📋 What is an ADR?\r\nArchitecture Decision Records document important architectural decisions made for Fed-Dup.\r\n\r\n## 📅 ADR Index\r\n\r\n### ADR-001: No Database Architecture\r\n**Status**: ✅ Accepted\r\n**Date**: 2026-09-01\r\n\r\n#### Context\r\nFed-Dup needed a storage solution for repository configurations and credentials.\r\n\r\n#### Decision\r\nUse a single JSON file (`config.json`) instead of a database.\r\n\r\n#### Rationale\r\n- Simplicity - no database setup required\r\n- Portability - configuration is a single file\r\n- Ease of backup - just copy the file\r\n- Perfect for small-scale deployments\r\n\r\n#### Consequences\r\n- Limited to single-node deployments\r\n- No concurrent write support\r\n- Must handle file locking for race conditions\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n### ADR-002: Python + Streamlit for UI\r\n**Status**: ✅ Accepted\r\n**Date**: 2026-09-01\r\n\r\n#### Context\r\nNeed a UI for managing Fed-Dup configuration and triggering syncs.\r\n\r\n#### Decision\r\nUse Python with Streamlit framework.\r\n\r\n#### Rationale\r\n- Python ecosystem for Git integration\r\n- Streamlit\'s simplicity\r\n- No separate frontend stack required\r\n- Fast development velocity\r\n- Built-in theming and components\r\n\r\n#### Consequences\r\n- Web UI only (no mobile app)\r\n- Requires Streamlit server to run\r\n- Limited to Python 3.7+\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n### ADR-003: Git Subprocess for Mirroring\r\n**Status**: ✅ Accepted\r\n**Date**: 2026-09-01\r\n\r\n#### Context\r\nNeed to perform Git mirroring operations.\r\n\r\n#### Decision\r\nUse subprocess to call Git CLI directly.\r\n\r\n#### Rationale\r\n- Git CLI is battle-tested and robust\r\n- No need for a Git library dependency\r\n- Full Git feature support\r\n- Works with any Git version\r\n\r\n#### Consequences\r\n- Git must be installed\r\n- Process management overhead\r\n- Error handling via stderr parsing\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n### ADR-004: Docker-First Deployment\r\n**Status**: ✅ Accepted\r\n**Date**: 2026-09-01\r\n\r\n#### Context\r\nNeed a simple, reliable deployment method.\r\n\r\n#### Decision\r\nProvide Docker and Docker Compose as primary deployment method.\r\n\r\n#### Rationale\r\n- Consistent environment\r\n- Easy to deploy on any platform\r\n- Self-contained with all dependencies\r\n- Easy updates and rollbacks\r\n\r\n#### Consequences\r\n- Larger image size\r\n- Docker knowledge required\r\n- Additional resource usage\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n### ADR-005: Background Worker + UI Separation\r\n**Status**: ✅ Accepted\r\n**Date**: 2026-09-01\r\n\r\n#### Context\r\nNeed automatic sync without manual UI interaction.\r\n\r\n#### Decision\r\nSeparate background worker process from Streamlit UI.\r\n\r\n#### Rationale\r\n- UI and sync operations are independent\r\n- Worker can run in background\r\n- Can be scaled separately\r\n- UI remains responsive during syncs\r\n\r\n#### Consequences\r\n- Two processes to manage\r\n- Shared config file access\r\n- Potential for race conditions\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 🔄 ADR Process\r\n\r\n### Creating a New ADR\r\n1. Copy template from ADR-XXX\r\n2. Fill in context, decision, rationale\r\n3. Discuss in issue/pull request\r\n4. Merge when consensus reached\r\n\r\n### ADR Template\r\n```markdown\r\n# ADR-XXX: [Title]\r\n\r\n**Status**: Proposed/Accepted/Deprecated\r\n**Date**: YYYY-MM-DD\r\n\r\n#### Context\r\n[What\'s the situation?]\r\n\r\n#### Decision\r\n[What did we decide?]\r\n\r\n#### Rationale\r\n[Why did we choose this?]\r\n\r\n#### Consequences\r\n[What are the trade-offs?]\r\nDocument maintained by Fed-Dup team 🛡️\r\n\r\ntext\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n### 27. `docs/ROADMAP.md`\r\n\r\n```markdown\r\n# Fed-Dup Roadmap\r\n\r\n## 🎯 Vision\r\nMake repository backup and duplication seamless, automatic, and accessible to every developer.\r\n\r\n## 🗺️ Roadmap\r\n\r\n### ✅ Completed (v1.0.0)\r\n- [x] Core mirroring engine\r\n- [x] Streamlit UI\r\n- [x] JSON configuration\r\n- [x] Docker deployment\r\n- [x] Background Docker Compose (Recommended)\r\n\r\n#### Quick Start\r\n```bash\r\ngit clone https://github.com/yourusername/fed-dup\r\ncd fed-dup\r\ncp config.json.example config.json\r\n# Edit config.json with your tokens\r\ndocker-compose up -d\r\nProduction Setup\r\nyaml\r\n# docker-compose.prod.yml\r\nversion: \'3.8\'\r\nservices:\r\n  feddup:\r\n    build: .\r\n    ports:\r\n      - "8501:8501"\r\n    volumes:\r\n      - ./config.json:/app/config.json:ro\r\n      - ./feddup_workspace:/app/feddup_workspace\r\n    environment:\r\n      - STREAMLIT_SERVER_MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE=200\r\n      - STREAMLIT_BROWSER_GATHER_USAGE_STATS=false\r\n    restart: unless-stopped\r\n    logging:\r\n      driver: "json-file"\r\n      options:\r\n        max-size: "10m"\r\n        max-file: "3"\r\n2. Local Installation\r\nRequirements\r\nPython 3.9+\r\n\r\nGit 2.25+\r\n\r\nInstall\r\nbash\r\n# Clone\r\ngit clone https://github.com/yourusername/fed-dup\r\ncd fed-dup\r\n\r\n# Install dependencies\r\npip install -r requirements.txt\r\n\r\n# Configure\r\ncp config.json.example config.json\r\n# Edit config.json\r\n\r\n# Run\r\nstreamlit run app.py &\r\npython -c "from worker import auto_sync_loop; auto_sync_loop()"\r\n3. Kubernetes Deployment\r\nyaml\r\n# k8s-deployment.yaml\r\napiVersion: apps/v1\r\nkind: Deployment\r\nmetadata:\r\n  name: feddup\r\n  namespace: feddup\r\nspec:\r\n  replicas: 1\r\n  selector:\r\n    matchLabels:\r\n      app: feddup\r\n  template:\r\n    metadata:\r\n      labels:\r\n        app: feddup\r\n    spec:\r\n      containers:\r\n      - name: feddup\r\n        image: feddup:latest\r\n        ports:\r\n        - containerPort: 8501\r\n        env:\r\n        - name: STREAMLIT_SERVER_PORT\r\n          value: "8501"\r\n        volumeMounts:\r\n        - name: config\r\n          mountPath: /app/config.json\r\n          subPath: config.json\r\n        - name: workspace\r\n          mountPath: /app/feddup_workspace\r\n      volumes:\r\n      - name: config\r\n        configMap:\r\n          name: feddup-config\r\n      - name: workspace\r\n        persistentVolumeClaim:\r\n          claimName: feddup-workspace\r\n4. Cloud Deployment\r\nAWS ECS\r\nbash\r\n# Build image\r\ndocker build -t feddup .\r\ndocker tag feddup:latest your-aws-account.dkr.ecr.region.amazonaws.com/feddup:latest\r\n\r\n# Push to ECR\r\naws ecr get-login-password | docker login --username AWS --password-stdin your-account.dkr.ecr.region.amazonaws.com\r\ndocker push your-aws-account.dkr.ecr.region.amazonaws.com/feddup:latest\r\n\r\n# Deploy to ECS\r\necs-cli compose --file docker-compose.yml up\r\nGoogle Cloud Run\r\nbash\r\n# Build and push to GCR\r\ngcloud builds submit --tag gcr.io/your-project/feddup\r\n\r\n# Deploy\r\ngcloud run deploy feddup \\\r\n  --image gcr.io/your-project/feddup \\\r\n  --platform managed \\\r\n  --port 8501 \\\r\n  --memory 1Gi \\\r\n  --cpu 1\r\n5. Synology NAS\r\nUsing Docker Package\r\nInstall Docker from Synology Package Center\r\n\r\nOpen Docker → Registry → Search "feddup"\r\n\r\nDownload the image\r\n\r\nCreate container with:\r\n\r\nPort mapping: 8501:8501\r\n\r\nVolume: /docker/feddup/config.json → /app/config.json\r\n\r\nVolume: /docker/feddup/workspace → /app/feddup_workspace\r\n\r\n🔧 Environment Variables\r\nVariable\tDescription\tDefault\r\nSTREAMLIT_SERVER_PORT\tUI port\t8501\r\nSTREAMLIT_SERVER_ADDRESS\tBind address\t0.0.0.0\r\nSTREAMLIT_BROWSER_GATHER_USAGE_STATS\tTelemetry\tfalse\r\nTZ\tTimezone\tUTC\r\n📊 Monitoring\r\nHealth Check\r\nbash\r\ncurl http://localhost:8501/_stcore/health\r\nLogs\r\nbash\r\n# Docker logs\r\ndocker logs feddup\r\n\r\n# K8s logs\r\nkubectl logs -f deployment/feddup\r\nMetrics\r\nSync success/failure rate\r\n\r\nRepository count\r\n\r\nWorkspace disk usage\r\n\r\nResponse time\r\n\r\n🔒 Security Best Practices\r\nNever commit config.json\r\n\r\nUse read-only tokens\r\n\r\nRun with least privilege\r\n\r\nRegularly rotate tokens\r\n\r\nMonitor access logs\r\n\r\nUse secrets management (Kubernetes Secrets, AWS Secrets Manager)\r\n\r\n🆘 Troubleshooting\r\nCommon Issues\r\nPort already in use: Change port in docker-compose.yml\r\n\r\nGit not found: Install Git in container\r\n\r\nPermission denied: Check volume mounts\r\n\r\nToken expired: Refresh tokens in config.json\r\n\r\nDebug Mode\r\nbash\r\nstreamlit run app.py --logger.level=debug\r\nNeed help? Open an issue or discussion! 🛡️\r\n\r\ntext\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n### 29. `docs/BUILD.md`\r\n\r\n```markdown\r\n# Building Fed-Dup\r\n\r\n## 🏗️ Build Options\r\n\r\n### 1. Build from Source\r\n\r\n#### Prerequisites\r\n- Python 3.9+\r\n- Git\r\n- Docker (optional)\r\n\r\n#### Build Python Package\r\n```bash\r\n# Clone\r\ngit clone https://github.com/yourusername/fed-dup\r\ncd fed-dup\r\n\r\n# Install build tools\r\npip install build wheel twine\r\n\r\n# Build\r\npython -m build\r\n\r\n# Check\r\ntwine check dist/*\r\nBuild Docker Image\r\nbash\r\n# Build image\r\ndocker build -t feddup:latest .\r\n\r\n# Build with specific tag\r\ndocker build -t feddup:v1.0.0 .\r\n\r\n# Build for multiple architectures\r\ndocker buildx build --platform linux/amd64,linux/arm64 -t feddup:latest .\r\n2. Development Build\r\nSetup Development Environment\r\nbash\r\n# Clone\r\ngit clone https://github.com/yourusername/fed-dup\r\ncd fed-dup\r\n\r\n# Create virtual environment\r\npython -m venv venv\r\nsource venv/bin/activate  # On Windows: venv\\Scripts\\activate\r\n\r\n# Install in development mode\r\npip install -e .\r\n\r\n# Install dev dependencies\r\npip install -r dev-requirements.txt\r\nRun in Development Mode\r\nbash\r\n# Run Streamlit with hot reload\r\nstreamlit run app.py\r\n\r\n# Run worker\r\npython worker.py\r\n3. CI/CD Build\r\nGitHub Actions\r\nThe CI pipeline automatically builds on every push:\r\n\r\n.github/workflows/build.yml\r\n\r\n.github/workflows/ci.yml\r\n\r\nLocal CI Simulation\r\nbash\r\n# Run all CI checks locally\r\npytest tests/ -v\r\nflake8 feddup/ app.py worker.py\r\nblack --check feddup/ app.py worker.py\r\nmypy feddup/\r\n4. Cross-Platform Build\r\nWindows\r\npowershell\r\n# Build with pyinstaller\r\npip install pyinstaller\r\npyinstaller --onefile app.py\r\nmacOS\r\nbash\r\n# Build macOS app\r\npip install py2app\r\npython setup.py py2app\r\n5. Docker Build Optimization\r\nMulti-stage Build\r\ndockerfile\r\n# builder stage\r\nFROM python:3.11-slim AS builder\r\nCOPY requirements.txt .\r\nRUN pip install --user -r requirements.txt\r\n\r\n# final stage\r\nFROM python:3.11-slim\r\nCOPY --from=builder /root/.local /root/.local\r\nCOPY . .\r\nCMD streamlit run app.py\r\n6. Build Artifacts\r\nArtifact\tLocation\tPurpose\r\nPython Wheel\tdist/*.whl\tPyPI installation\r\nDocker Image\tfeddup:latest\tContainerized deployment\r\nSource Distribution\tdist/*.tar.gz\tSource code archive\r\nDocumentation\tdocs/_build/\tHTML docs\r\n7. Build Verification\r\nTest Build\r\nbash\r\n# Test Python package\r\npython -c "import feddup; print(\'OK\')"\r\n\r\n# Test Docker image\r\ndocker run --rm feddup:latest python -c "import feddup; print(\'OK\')"\r\n\r\n# Run tests\r\npytest tests/ -v\r\nSmoke Test\r\nbash\r\n# Start service\r\ndocker-compose up -d\r\n\r\n# Check health\r\ncurl http://localhost:8501/_stcore/health\r\n\r\n# Test sync\r\npython -c "from feddup.engine import duplicate_repository; print(\'Engine loaded\')"\r\n8. Optimizing Build Size\r\nPython Package\r\nbash\r\n# Remove unnecessary files\r\nfind . -name "*.pyc" -delete\r\nfind . -name "__pycache__" -delete\r\nDocker Image\r\nbash\r\n# Use slim base image\r\nFROM python:3.11-slim\r\n\r\n# Combine RUN commands\r\nRUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y git && \\\r\n    apt-get clean && \\\r\n    rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*\r\n9. Build Troubleshooting\r\nCommon Issues\r\nGit not found in Docker\r\n\r\ndockerfile\r\nRUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y git\r\nPermission denied for workspace\r\n\r\nbash\r\nchmod 755 feddup_workspace/\r\nPort already in use\r\n\r\nbash\r\n# Change port\r\nSTREAMLIT_SERVER_PORT=8502 streamlit run app.py\r\nImport errors\r\n\r\nbash\r\n# Install in development mode\r\npip install -e .\r\n10. Build Automation\r\nMakefile\r\nmakefile\r\n.PHONY: build test clean docker\r\n\r\nbuild:\r\n\tpython -m build\r\n\r\ntest:\r\n\tpytest tests/ -v\r\n\r\ndocker:\r\n\tdocker build -t feddup:latest .\r\n\r\nclean:\r\n\trm -rf build/ dist/ *.egg-info\r\n\tfind . -name "*.pyc" -delete\r\n\tfind . -name "__pycache__" -delete\r\n\r\nall: clean build test docker\r\nBuilt with ❤️ by the Fed-Dup team 🛡️\r\n\r\ntext\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n### 30. `docs/INSTALL.md`\r\n\r\n```markdown\r\n# Installing Fed-Dup\r\n\r\n## 📦 Installation Methods\r\n\r\n### 1. --server.address=0.0.0.0\r\nSynology NAS\r\nInstall Docker from Package Center\r\n\r\nPull feddup:latest\r\n\r\nCreate container with port 8501 and volumes\r\n\r\n🔧 (macOS)\r\nlaunchctl load ~/Library/LaunchAgents/feddup.plist\r\n\r\n# Windows Service\r\nsc create feddup binPath="python worker.py"\r\n🐳 Container Installation\r\nKubernetes\r\nbash\r\n# Install GITHUB_TOKEN=ghp_xxxxx\r\nexport BACKUP_TOKEN=glpat_xxxxx\r\nexport REPOS=\'[{"name":"test","source":"https://github.com/...","destination":"https://gitlab.com/..."}]\'\r\n✅ Verification\r\nCheck Access\r\ntext\r\nhttp://localhost:8501\r\n🆘 Troubleshooting\r\nCommon Issues\r\nPort Already in Use\r\nbash\r\n# Use different port\r\nstreamlit run app.py --server.port 8502\r\nPermission install git     # Windows\r\nConfiguration Invalid\r\nbash\r\n# Validate config\r\npython -m feddup validate-config\r\n🗑️ Uninstall\r\nDocker\r\nbash\r\ndocker stop feddup\r\ndocker rm duplication engine designed to protect your code by automatically mirroring Git repositories to backup platforms.\r\n\r\n## 📋 Quick Facts\r\n\r\n| Attribute | Value |\r\n|-----------|-------|\r\n| **Type** | Repository Mirroring Tool |\r\n| **Language** | Python |\r\n| **UI** | Streamlit |\r\n| **Deployment** | Docker, Local, Cloud |\r\n| **License** | MIT |\r\n| **Maintainer** | Your Name |\r\n\r\n## 🏗️ Architecture\r\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\r\n│ Fed-Dup │\r\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\r\n│ ┌─────────────┐ ┌─────────────┐ ┌────────────┐ │\r\n│ │ Streamlit │ │ Worker │ │ Config │ │\r\n│ │ UI Layer │ │ (Async) │ │ (JSON) │ │\r\n│ └─────────────┘ └─────────────┘ └────────────┘ │\r\n│ ┌─────────────┐ ┌─────────────┐ │\r\n│ │ Git │ │ Security │ │\r\n│ │ Engine │ │ Layer │ │\r\n│ └─────────────┘ └─────────────┘ │\r\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\r\n\r\ntext\r\n\r\n## 🚀 Features\r\n\r\n### Core (Self-Hosted)\r\n\r\n| Platform | Estimated Cost |\r\n|----------|----------------|\r\n| Home Server | $0 (existing) |\r\n| VPS (DigitalOcean) | $5-10/month |\r\n| AWS EC2 (t3.micro) | $8/month |\r\n| Raspberry Pi | $0 (one-time) |\r\n\r\n## 🔄 Migration Savings\r\n\r\n### Moving from Paid Service\r\n1. **Setup Fed-Dup** (15 minutes)\r\n2. **Configure your repos** (5 minutes)\r\n3. **Cancel paid service** (2 minutes)\r\n4. **Save $10-100/month**\r\n\r\n## 🎁 Contributors\r\n\r\nContributors get:\r\n- 🏆 Name in AUTHORS.md\r\n- 🌟 Special thanks in README\r\n- 💜 Community recognition\r\n- 🔑 Maintainer access (for active contributors)\r\n\r\n## ❓ FAQ\r\n\r\n**Is Fed-Dup really free?**\r\nYes, completely free and open source (MIT License).\r\n\r\n**Can I use it commercially?**\r\nAbsolutely! MIT License allows commercial use.\r\n\r\n**What about support?**\r\nCommunity support via GitHub Issues and Discussions.\r\n\r\n**Will there be paid features?**\r\nAll core features will remain free. Future cloud services may have paid tiers.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n**Start protecting your code for free today! 🛡️**\r\n34. docs/COPYING.md\r\nmarkdown\r\n# Fed-Dup License\r\n\r\n## 📄 MIT License\r\n\r\nCopyright (c) 2026 Your Name\r\n\r\nPermission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy\r\nof this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal\r\nin the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights\r\nto use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell\r\ncopies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is\r\nfurnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:\r\n\r\nThe above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all\r\ncopies or substantial portions of the Software.\r\n\r\nTHE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR\r\nIMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY,\r\nFITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. At least 5 significant contributions\r\n2. Active for 6+ months\r\n3. Community vote\r\n4. BDFL approval\r\n\r\n## 🔄 Release Process\r\n\r\n### Versioning\r\n- **MAJOR**: Breaking changes\r\n- **MINOR**: New features (backwards compatible)\r\n- **PATCH**: Bug fixes\r\n\r\n### Release Stages\r\n1. **Alpha**: Internal testing\r\n2. **Beta**: Public testing\r\n3. **Release Candidate**: Final testing\r\n4. **Stable**: Official release\r\n\r\n### Release Manager\r\n- **Primary**: Your Name\r\n- **Backup**: *To be appointed*\r\n\r\n## 🛡️ Security Policy\r\n\r\n### Reporting\r\n1. Private security advisory\r\n2. Core team investigates\r\n3. Patch created\r\n4. Coordinated disclosure\r\n\r\n### Response Time\r\n- **Critical**: 24 hours\r\n- **High**: 48 hours\r\n- **Medium**: 7 days\r\n- **Low**: 30 days\r\n\r\n## 💰 Financial Governance\r\n\r\n### Funding Sources\r\n- Donations (Ko-fi, GitHub Sponsors)\r\n- Grants (OSI, NLnet)\r\n- Corporate sponsors\r\n\r\n### Fund Usage\r\n- Infrastructure costs (CI, hosting)\r\n- Developer time (bounties)\r\n- Community events\r\n- Documentation improvements\r\n\r\n### Transparency\r\nAll financial decisions and expenses are publicly documented.\r\n\r\n## 📝 Code of Conduct\r\n\r\nFed-Dup follows the [Contributor Covenant Code of Conduct](CODE_OF_CONDUCT.md).\r\n\r\n### Enforcement\r\n1. **Report**: Contact maintainers\r\n2. **Investigate**: Core team reviews\r\n3. **Action**: Warning, suspension, or ban\r\n\r\n## 🌐 Community Engagement\r\n\r\n### Communication Channels\r\n- **GitHub Issues**: Bug reports\r\n- **GitHub Discussions**: General chat\r\n- **Discord/Slack**: Real-time chat (if applicable)\r\n- **Twitter**: Announcements\r\n\r\n### Events\r\n- Monthly community meetings (virtual)\r\n- Annual contributors summit (virtual)\r\n\r\n## 🔄 Governance Evolution\r\n\r\nThis document evolves with the project. Update document\r\n\r\n## 📊 Project Health\r\n\r\n### Metrics Tracked\r\n- Active contributors\r\n- Issue resolution time\r\n- PR merge rate\r\n- Community growth\r\n- User satisfaction\r\n\r\n### Annual Review\r\n- Governance review\r\n- Roadmap update\r\n- Budget review\r\n- Contributor recognition\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n**Governed by the community, for the community 🛡️**\r\n37. `CODE_OF_CONDUCT.md`\r\n\r\n```markdown\r\n# Contributor Covenant Code of Conduct\r\n\r\n## Our Pledge\r\n\r\nWe as members, contributors, and leaders pledge to make participation in our\r\ncommunity a harassment-free experience for everyone, regardless of age, body\r\nsize, visible or invisible disability, ethnicity, sex characteristics, gender\r\nidentity and expression, level of experience, education, socio-economic status,\r\nnationality, personal appearance, race, religion, or sexual identity\r\nand orientation.\r\n\r\nWe pledge to act and interact in ways that contribute to an open, welcoming,\r\ndiverse, inclusive, and healthy community.\r\n\r\n## Our Standards\r\n\r\nExamples of behavior that contributes to a positive environment:\r\n\r\n* Demonstrating empathy and kindness toward other people\r\n* Being respectful of differing opinions, viewpoints, and experiences\r\n* Giving and gracefully accepting constructive feedback\r\n* Accepting responsibility and apologizing to those affected by our mistakes\r\n* Focusing on what is best for the overall community\r\n\r\nExamples of unacceptable behavior:\r\n\r\n* The use of sexualized language or imagery, and sexual attention or advances\r\n* Trolling, insulting or derogatory comments, and personal or political attacks\r\n* Public or private harassment\r\n* Publishing others\' private information without explicit permission\r\n* Other conduct which could reasonably be considered inappropriate\r\n\r\n## Enforcement Responsibilities\r\n\r\nProject maintainers are responsible for clarifying and enforcing our standards of\r\nacceptable behavior and will take appropriate and fair corrective action in\r\nresponse to any behavior that they deem inappropriate, threatening, offensive,\r\nor harmful.\r\n\r\nProject maintainers have the right and responsibility to remove, edit, or reject\r\ncomments, commits, code, wiki edits, issues, and other contributions that are\r\nnot aligned to this Code of Conduct, and will communicate reasons for moderation\r\ndecisions when appropriate.\r\n\r\n## Scope\r\n\r\nThis Code of Conduct applies within all community spaces, and also applies when\r\nan individual is officially representing the community in public spaces.\r\nExamples of representing our community include using an official e-mail address,\r\nposting via an official social media account, or acting as an appointed\r\nrepresentative at an online or offline event.\r\n\r\n## Enforcement\r\n\r\nInstances of abusive, harassing, or otherwise unacceptable behavior may be\r\nreported to the project team at your@email.com. All complaints will be reviewed\r\nand investigated and will result in a response that is deemed necessary and\r\nappropriate to the circumstances. The project team is obligated to maintain\r\nconfidentiality with regard to the reporter of an incident.\r\n\r\n## Enforcement Guidelines\r\n\r\nProject maintainers will follow these Community Impact Guidelines in determining\r\nthe consequences for any action they deem in violation of this Code of Conduct:\r\n\r\n### 1. Correction\r\n**Community Impact**: Use of inappropriate language or other behavior deemed\r\nunprofessional or unwelcome in the community.\r\n\r\n**Consequence**: A private, written warning from maintainers, providing clarity\r\naround the nature of the violation and an explanation of why the behavior was\r\ninappropriate. A public apology may be requested.\r\n\r\n### 2. Warning\r\n**Community Impact**: A violation through a single incident or series of actions.\r\n\r\n**Consequence**: A warning with consequences for continued behavior. No\r\ninteraction with the people involved, including unsolicited interaction with\r\nthose enforcing the Code of Conduct, for a specified period of time. This\r\nincludes avoiding interactions in community spaces as well as external channels\r\nlike social media. Violating these terms may lead to a temporary or permanent ban.\r\n\r\n### 3. Temporary Ban\r\n**Community Impact**: A serious violation of community standards, including\r\nsustained inappropriate behavior.\r\n\r\n**Consequence**: A temporary ban from any sort of interaction or public\r\ncommunication with the community for a specified period of time. No public or\r\nprivate interaction with the people involved, including unsolicited interaction\r\nwith those enforcing the Code of Conduct, is allowed during this period.\r\nViolating these terms may lead to a permanent ban.\r\n\r\n### 4. Permanent Ban\r\n**Community Impact**: Demonstrating a pattern of violation of community\r\nstandards, including sustained inappropriate behavior, harassment of an\r\nindividual, or aggression toward or disparagement of classes of individuals.\r\n\r\n**Consequence**: A permanent ban from any sort of public interaction within\r\nthe community.\r\n\r\n## Attribution\r\n\r\nThis Code of Conduct is adapted from the [Contributor Covenant][homepage],\r\nversion 2.0, available at\r\nhttps://www.contributor-covenant.org/version/2/0/code_of_conduct.html.\r\n\r\nCommunity Impact Guidelines were inspired by [Mozilla\'s code of conduct\r\nenforcement ladder](https://github.com/mozilla/diversity).\r\n\r\n[homepage]: https://www.contributor-covenant.org\r\n\r\nFor answers to common questions about this code of conduct, see the FAQ at\r\nhttps://www.contributor-covenant.org/faq. Translations are available at\r\nhttps://www.contributor-covenant.org/translations.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n**Be kind, be respectful, build together! 🛡️**\r\n39. CONTRIBUTING.md\r\nmarkdown\r\n# Contributing to Fed-Dup\r\n\r\nThank you for your interest in contributing to Fed-Dup! 🛡️\r\n\r\n## 📋 Table of Contents\r\n\r\n- [Code of Conduct](#code-of-conduct)\r\n- [Getting Started](#getting-started)\r\n- [Development Workflow](#development-workflow)\r\n- [Code Style](#code-style)\r\n- [Testing](#testing)\r\n- [Documentation](#documentation)\r\n- [Pull Request Process](#pull-request-process)\r\n- [Feature Requests](#feature-requests)\r\n- [Bug Reports](#bug-reports)\r\n- [Community](#community)\r\n\r\n## Code of Conduct\r\n\r\nThis project follows the [Contributor Covenant Code of Conduct](CODE_OF_CONDUCT.md).\r\nPlease read and follow it.\r\n\r\n## Getting Started\r\n\r\n### Prerequisites\r\n- Python 3.9+\r\n- Git\r\n- Basic understanding of Git operations\r\n\r\n### Fork & Clone\r\n```bash\r\n# Fork the repository on GitHub\r\n# Then clone your fork\r\ngit clone https://github.com/yourusername/fed-dup\r\ncd fed-dup\r\n\r\n# Add upstream remote\r\ngit remote add upstream https://github.com/yourusername/fed-dup\r\nSetup Development Environment\r\nbash\r\n# Create virtual environment\r\npython -m venv venv\r\nsource venv/bin/activate  # On Windows: venv\\Scripts\\activate\r\n\r\n# Install dependencies\r\npip install -r requirements.txt\r\npip install -r dev-requirements.txt  # If exists\r\n\r\n# Install in development mode\r\npip install -e .\r\nDevelopment Workflow\r\n1. Create a Branch\r\nbash\r\ngit checkout -b feature/your-feature-name\r\n# or\r\ngit checkout -b fix/issue-description\r\n2. Make Changes\r\nWrite code\r\n\r\nAdd tests\r\n\r\nUpdate documentation\r\n\r\nFollow code style\r\n\r\n3. Test Your Changes\r\nbash\r\n# Run tests\r\npytest tests/ -v\r\n\r\n# Check coverage\r\npytest --cov=feddup tests/\r\n\r\n# Lint\r\nflake8 feddup/ app.py worker.py\r\n\r\n# Format\r\nblack feddup/ app.py worker.py\r\n4. Commit Changes\r\nbash\r\ngit add .\r\ngit commit -m "feat: Add new feature"\r\n\r\n# Follow Conventional Commits format:\r\n# feat: New feature\r\n# fix: Bug fix\r\n# docs: Documentation\r\n# style: Code style\r\n# refactor: Refactoring\r\n# test: Tests\r\n# chore: Maintenance\r\n5. Push & Create PR\r\nbash\r\ngit push origin feature/your-feature-name\r\n# Open PR on GitHub\r\nCode Style\r\nPython\r\nFormatter: Black\r\n\r\nLinter: Flake8\r\n\r\nType hints: Use mypy\r\n\r\npython\r\n# Good\r\ndef duplicate_repository(repo: Dict[str, str], token: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:\r\n    """Duplicate a repository."""\r\n    pass\r\n\r\n# Bad\r\ndef dup(repo, token):\r\n    pass\r\nImports\r\npython\r\n# Standard library\r\nimport os\r\nimport sys\r\n\r\n# Third-party\r\nimport streamlit as st\r\n\r\n# Local\r\nfrom feddup.engine import duplicate_repository\r\nDocumentation\r\nUse docstrings for functions and classes\r\n\r\nInclude type hints\r\n\r\nDocument parameters and returns\r\n\r\nTesting\r\nWriting Tests\r\npython\r\n# tests/test_engine.py\r\nimport pytest\r\nfrom feddup.engine import duplicate_repository\r\n\r\ndef test_duplicate_repository():\r\n    # Arrange\r\n    repo = {"name": "test", "source": "...", "destination": "..."}\r\n    \r\n    # Act\r\n    success, msg = duplicate_repository(repo, "token", "token")\r\n    \r\n    # Assert\r\n    assert success is True\r\nRunning Tests\r\nbash\r\n# Run all tests\r\npytest\r\n\r\n# Run specific test\r\npytest tests/test_engine.py\r\n\r\n# Run with coverage\r\npytest --cov=feddup tests/\r\nDocumentation\r\nREADME Updates\r\nUpdate features list\r\n\r\nAdd examples\r\n\r\nUpdate installation instructions\r\n\r\nAPI Documentation\r\nDocument all public functions\r\n\r\nInclude examples\r\n\r\nExplain parameters\r\n\r\nExamples\r\nProvide `usage.md`\r\n\r\n```markdown\r\n# Fed-Dup Usage Guide\r\n\r\n## 🚀 Getting Started\r\n\r\n### 1. Launch the UI\r\n```bash\r\nstreamlit run app.py\r\nNavigate to http://localhost:8501\r\n\r\n2. Configure Credentials\r\nGo to Settings sidebar\r\n\r\nEnter GitHub PAT\r\n\r\nEnter Backup Platform Token\r\n\r\nClick "Save Credentials"\r\n\r\n3. Add Repositories\r\nClick "Add Repository"\r\n\r\nEnter a friendly name\r\n\r\nEnter source URL (GitHub)\r\n\r\nEnter destination URL (backup)\r\n\r\nClick "Add to Fed-Dup"\r\n\r\n4. Sync Repositories\r\nManual: Click "Sync" button\r\n\r\nAuto: Worker runs hourly\r\n\r\nAll: Click "Sync All Now"\r\n\r\n🎯 Common Use Cases\r\nPersonal Backup\r\njson\r\n{\r\n  "name": "my-project",\r\n  "source": "https://github.com/username/my-project.git",\r\n  "destination": "https://gitlab.com/username/my-project.git"\r\n}\r\nOrganization Backup\r\njson\r\n{\r\n  "name": "org-repo",\r\n  "source": "https://github.com/orgname/repo.git",\r\n  "destination": "https://gitea.company.com/orgname/repo.git"\r\n}\r\nMultiple Destinations\r\njson\r\n[\r\n  {\r\n    "name": "project-1",\r\n    "source": "https://github.com/user/project1.git",\r\n    "destination": "https://gitlab.com/user/project1.git"\r\n  },\r\n  {\r\n    "name": "project-2",\r\n    "source": "https://github.com/user/project2.git",\r\n    "destination": "https://codeberg.org/user/project2.git"\r\n  }\r\n]\r\n🛠️ Advanced Usage\r\nCLI Mode\r\nbash\r\n# Run sync from CLI\r\npython -c "\r\nfrom feddup.engine import duplicate_repository\r\nrepo = {\'name\': \'test\', \'source\': \'...\', \'destination\': \'...\'}\r\nsuccess, msg = duplicate_repository(repo, \'token1\', \'token2\')\r\nprint(msg)\r\n"\r\nCustom Sync Intervals\r\njson\r\n{\r\n  "settings": {\r\n    "auto_sync_interval": 1800  # 30 minutes\r\n  }\r\n}\r\nCleanup After Sync\r\njson\r\n{\r\n  "settings": {\r\n    "cleanup_after_sync": true\r\n  }\r\n}\r\nParallel Syncs\r\njson\r\n{\r\n  "settings": {\r\n    "max_parallel_syncs": 5\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n📊 Monitoring\r\nCheck Sync Status\r\nOpen Fed-Dup UI\r\n\r\nView sync status per repo\r\n\r\nCheck logs:\r\n\r\nbash\r\ndocker logs feddup\r\n# or\r\ntail -f feddup.log\r\nHealth Check\r\nbash\r\ncurl http://localhost:8501/_stcore/health\r\nMetrics\r\nRepository count\r\n\r\nLast sync time\r\n\r\nWorkspace size\r\n\r\nSync success rate\r\n\r\n🔧 Troubleshooting\r\nSync Fails\r\nCheck tokens are valid\r\n\r\nVerify URLs are correct\r\n\r\nCheck network connectivity\r\n\r\nReview error message\r\n\r\nToken Expired\r\nGenerate new token\r\n\r\nUpdate config.json\r\n\r\nSave credentials in UI\r\n\r\nRetry sync\r\n\r\nDisk Full\r\nEnable cleanup\r\n\r\nRemove old workspace\r\n\r\nIncrease disk space\r\n\r\n🐳 Docker Usage\r\nRun in Background\r\nbash\r\ndocker-compose up -d\r\nView Logs\r\nbash\r\ndocker logs -f feddup\r\nUpdate\r\nbash\r\ndocker-compose pull\r\ndocker-compose up -d\r\nStop\r\nbash\r\ndocker-compose down\r\n🔗 Integration Examples\r\nPre-commit Hook\r\nbash\r\n#!/bin/bash\r\n# .git/hooks/pre-commit\r\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:8501/sync\r\nCI/CD Integration\r\nyaml\r\n# .github/workflows/backup.yml\r\nname: Backup to Fed-Dup\r\non:\r\n  push:\r\n    branches: [ main ]\r\njobs:\r\n  backup:\r\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\r\n    steps:\r\n      - run: curl -X POST http://feddup:8501/sync\r\nCron Job\r\ncron\r\n# Sync every 6 hours\r\n0 */6 * * * curl -X POST http://localhost:8501/sync\r\n📈 Best Practices\r\nUse read-only tokens when possible\r\n\r\nRegularly rotate tokens\r\n\r\nMonitor disk space\r\n\r\nTest restore process\r\n\r\nKeep config.json secure\r\n\r\nUse separate tokens for each platform\r\n\r\nBackup config.json itself\r\n\r\n🎨 UI Customization\r\nDark Mode\r\npython\r\n# Add to app.py\r\nst.set_page_config(\r\n    page_title="Fed-Dup",\r\n    page_icon="🛡️",\r\n    layout="wide",\r\n    initial_sidebar_state="expanded"\r\n)\r\nCustom CSS\r\ncss\r\n/* styles.css */\r\n.main-header {\r\n    color: #00cc88;\r\n}\r\nLoad with:\r\n\r\npython\r\nwith open(\'styles.css\') as f:\r\n    st.markdown(f\'<style>{f.read()}</style>\', unsafe_allow_html=True)\r\n❓ FAQ\r\nQ: How often does Fed-Dup sync?\r\nA: Every hour by default. Can be changed in config.\r\n\r\nQ: Does Fed-Dup store my tokens?\r\nA: Yes, in config.json on your server.\r\n\r\nQ: Can I use SSH instead of HTTPS?\r\nA: Coming soon in v1.1.0.\r\n\r\nQ: Does Fed-Dup back up issues/PRs?\r\nA: Coming soon in v1.2.0.\r\n\r\nQ: Is Fed-Dup free?\r\nA: Yes, MIT License - completely free.\r\n\r\nStart protecting your repositories today!
