FAQ.md\r\nmarkdown\r\n# Frequently Asked Questions\r\n\r\n## 🛡️ About Fed-Dup\r\n\r\n### What is Fed-Dup?\r\nFed-Dup is a federated repository duplication engine that mirrors Git repositories from GitHub to backup platforms like GitLab, Codeberg, or any Git server.\r\n\r\n### Why do I need Fed-Dup?\r\n- 📦 Protect against platform downtime\r\n- 🔄 Have a backup if GitHub goes down\r\n- 🎯 Prepare for platform migration\r\n- 🛡️ Meet compliance requirements\r\n\r\n### Is Fed-Dup free?\r\nYes! MIT License - completely free for personal and commercial use.\r\n\r\n## 🔧 Technical Questions\r\n\r\n### What platforms are supported?\r\n- **Source**: GitHub (others coming soon)\r\n- **Destination**: Any HTTPS Git server\r\n\r\n### Does Fed-Dup back up issues and PRs?\r\nNot yet. Coming in v1.2.0 (Q1 2027).\r\n\r\n### Do I need a database?\r\nNo! Fed-Dup uses a single JSON file for configuration.\r\n\r\n### What languages is Fed-Dup written in?\r\nPython (backend) with Streamlit (UI).\r\n\r\n### How does Fed-Dup work?\r\n1. Reads config.json for repos and tokens\r\n2. Executes `git clone --mirror` from source\r\n3. Executes `git push --mirror` to destination\r\n4. Cleans up workspace (optional)\r\n\r\n## 🔐 Security Questions\r\n\r\n### Are my tokens safe?\r\nTokens are stored only in config.json on your machine. Never displayed in UI or logs.\r\n\r\n### What permissions do tokens need?\r\n- **GitHub**: `repo` scope\r\n- **GitLab**: `api` scope\r\n\r\n### Can I use SSH instead of HTTPS?\r\nComing soon in v1.1.0.\r\n\r\n### Is HTTPS required?\r\nYes. SSH support is planned.\r\n\r\n## 🐳 Deployment Questions\r\n\r\n### Can I run Fed-Dup without Docker?\r\nYes. Install with pip or run from source.\r\n\r\n### What are the system requirements?\r\n- Python 3.9+\r\n- Git\r\n- 512MB RAM minimum\r\n- 1GB disk space (depends on repos)\r\n\r\n### Can I deploy on a Raspberry Pi?\r\nYes! Fed-Dup works on ARM.\r\n\r\n### Is there a cloud-hosted version?\r\nComing soon (Fed-Dup Cloud).\r\n\r\n## 🚀 Usage Questions\r\n\r\n### How often does Fed-Dup sync?\r\nEvery hour by default. Configurable.\r\n\r\n### Can I sync manually?\r\nYes. Click "Sync Now" in the UI.\r\n\r\n### What happens if a sync fails?\r\nFed-Dup logs the error and continues. Failed repos are marked.\r\n\r\n### Can I sync multiple repos at once?\r\nYes. Click "Sync All Now" to sync all repositories.\r\n\r\n### Does Fed-Dup handle large repos?\r\nYes, but large repos may need more resources.\r\n\r\n## 💡 Troubleshooting\r\n\r\n### Sync fails with authentication error\r\n1. Check tokens are valid\r\n2. Verify token permissions\r\n3. Update config.json\r\n4. Restart Fed-Dup\r\n\r\n### "Git not found" error\r\nInstall Git:\r\n```bash\r\n# Ubuntu\r\nsudo apt install git\r\n# macOS\r\nbrew install git\r\n# Windows\r\nchoco install git\r\nPort 8501 already in use\r\nChange port:\r\n\r\nbash\r\nstreamlit run app.py --server.port 8502\r\nDocker container won\'t start\r\nCheck logs: docker logs feddup\r\n\r\nVerify config.json exists\r\n\r\nCheck permissions on volumes\r\n\r\n🤝 Community Questions\r\nHow can I contribute?\r\nFork the repo\r\n\r\nMake changes\r\n\r\nSubmit PR\r\n\r\nSee CONTRIBUTING.md\r\n\r\nWhere can I get help?\r\n📖 Documentation\r\n\r\n💬 GitHub Discussions\r\n\r\n🐛 GitHub Issues\r\n\r\nIs there a community chat?\r\nComing soon (Discord/Slack).\r\n\r\nHow do I report a security issue?\r\nEmail security@fed-dup.org or use GitHub Security Advisory.\r\n\r\n📊 Statistics\r\nHow many repos can Fed-Dup handle?\r\nUnlimited.\r\n\r\nHow long does a sync take?\r\nDepends on repo size. Average: 1-5 minutes.\r\n\r\nDoes Fed-Dup compress data?\r\nNot yet. Planned for future release.\r\n\r\n🔮 Future Questions\r\nWhen will SSH support be added?\r\nTarget: v1.1.0 (Dec 2026).\r\n\r\nWhen will issues/PRs backup be added?\r\nTarget: v1.2.0 (Mar 2027).\r\n\r\nWill there be a CLI version?\r\nAlready exists! Use the worker script.\r\n\r\nCan I use Fed-Dup with Azure DevOps?\r\nNot yet. Support planned.\r\n\r\n💰 Financial Questions\r\nHow is Fed-Dup funded?\r\nDonations (Ko-fi, GitHub Sponsors)\r\n\r\nGrants\r\n\r\nCorporate sponsors\r\n\r\nCan I sponsor Fed-Dup?\r\nYes! See the Donations section in README.\r\n\r\nMore questions? Open a discussion! 🛡️\r\n\r\ntext\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n### 43. `NOTICE.md`\r\n\r\n```markdown\r\n# Fed-Dup Notices\r\n\r\n## 📋 Third-Party Notices\r\n\r\nFed-Dup includes or references the following third-party software:\r\n\r\n### Python & Streamlit\r\n- **Python**: Python Software Foundation License\r\n- **Streamlit**: Apache License 2.0\r\n\r\n### Git\r\n- **Git**: GNU General Public License v2\r\n\r\n### GitHub Actions\r\n- Various actions used in CI/CD workflows under their respective licenses\r\n\r\n### Docker\r\n- Docker Engine: Apache License 2.0\r\n- Docker Compose: Apache License 2.0\r\n\r\n## 📄 Licensing Summary\r\n\r\n| Component | License |\r\n|-----------|---------|\r\n| Fed-Dup (core code) | MIT |\r\n| Python | PSF License |\r\n| Streamlit | Apache 2.0 |\r\n| Git | GPL v2 |\r\n| Docker | Apache 2.0 |\r\n\r\n## 🔒 Security Notices\r\n\r\n### Security Advisories\r\nSecurity advisories are posted at:\r\nhttps://github.com/yourusername/fed-dup/security/advisories\r\n\r\n### Reporting Security Issues\r\n- Email: security@fed-dup.org\r\n- Or use GitHub Security Advisory\r\n\r\n### Vulnerability Disclosure\r\nWe follow responsible disclosure:\r\n1. SECURITY.md\r\nmarkdown\r\n# Security Policy\r\n\r\n## 🔒 Supported Versions\r\n\r\n| Version | Supported |\r\n|---------|-----------|\r\n| 1.0.x   | ✅ Active |\r\n| < 1.0   | ❌ Not supported |\r\n\r\n## 🛡️ Reporting a Vulnerability\r\n\r\n### Private Reporting\r\n**DO NOT** open public issues for security vulnerabilities.\r\n\r\nPlease report via:\r\n1. **GitHub Security Advisory**: Use the "Security" tab\r\n2. **Email**: `security@fed-dup.org`\r\n3. **Encrypted**: GPG key (available on request)\r\n\r\n### Response Timeline\r\n| Priority | Initial Response | Fix Release |\r\n|----------|------------------|-------------|\r\n| Critical | 24 hours | 7 days |\r\n| High | 48 hours | 14 days |\r\n| Medium | 7 days | 30 days |\r\n| Low | 30 days | 60 days |\r\n\r\n### What to Include\r\n1. Detailed description\r\n2. Steps to reproduce\r\n3. Affected versions\r\n4. Potential impact\r\n5. Suggested fix (if any)\r\n\r\n### Recognition\r\nWe publicly acknowledge security researchers who report vulnerabilities (with permission).\r\n\r\n## 🛡️ Security Best Practices\r\n\r\n### For Users\r\n1. **Use read-only tokens** when possible\r\n2. **Regularly rotate tokens** (every 90 days)\r\n3. **Store config.json securely**\r\n4. **Restrict access** to Fed-Dup instance\r\n5. **Enable HTTPS** for web UI\r\n6. **Monitor logs** for unauthorized access\r\n\r\n### For Developers\r\n1. **Never log tokens** or credentials\r\n2. **Validate all inputs** before processing\r\n3. **Use parameterized commands** (not shell)\r\n4. **Sanitize URLs** before Git operations\r\n5. **Scan dependencies** for vulnerabilities\r\n6. **Run security tests** in CI/CD\r\n\r\n## 🔐 Security Features\r\n\r\n### Current\r\n- ✅ Token sanitization in logs\r\n- ✅ URL validation\r\n- ✅ Secure credential storage (config.json)\r\n- ✅ No sensitive data in error messages\r\n- ✅ HTTPS support\r\n\r\n### Planned\r\n- 🔜 SSH key support\r\n- 🔜 Secret management integration\r\n- 🔜 Audit logging\r\n- 🔜 Rate limiting\r\n- 🔜 IP whitelisting\r\n\r\n## 🧪 Security Testing\r\n\r\n### Automated\r\n- CodeQL scanning (daily)\r\n- Dependency review (on PR)\r\n- Scorecards (weekly)\r\n- Bandit (on PR)\r\n\r\n### Manual\r\n- Penetration testing (quarterly)\r\n- Security code review (on major features)\r\n- Token rotation testing\r\n\r\n## 📋 Security Checklist\r\n\r\n### Before Release\r\n- [ ] Run security tests\r\n- [ ] Check for known vulnerabilities\r\n- [ ] Review dependency licenses\r\n- [ ] Test token rotation\r\n- [ ] Validate error handling\r\n\r\n### During Development\r\n- [ ] Never commit secrets\r\n- [ ] Use environment variables for testing\r\n- [ ] Sanitize all inputs\r\n- [ ] Review code for security issues\r\n- [ ] Update security documentation\r\n\r\n## 🔄 Vulnerability Database\r\n\r\nFed-Dup uses:\r\n- **GitHub Advisory Database**\r\n- **NVD (NIST)**\r\n- **OSV (Open Source Vulnerabilities)**\r\n\r\n## 📞 Security Contact\r\n\r\n### Primary\r\n- Email: security@fed-dup.org\r\n- GPG Key: [Key ID]\r\n\r\n### Backup\r\n- GitHub: [@yourusername](https://github.com/yourusername)\r\n- Matrix/Element: @yourusername:matrix.org\r\n\r\n## 📜 Security Policy History\r\n\r\n| Version | Date | Changes |\r\n|---------|------|---------|\r\n| 1.0.0 | 2026-09-01 | Initial security policy |\r\n| 1.1.0 | TBD | SSH support |\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n**Together, let\'s keep Fed-Dup secure! 🛡️**\r\n45. styles.css\r\ncss\r\n/* Fed-Dup Custom Styles */\r\n\r\n/* Main Header */\r\n.main-header {\r\n    font-size: 3rem;\r\n    font-weight: 700;\r\n    background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);\r\n    -webkit-background-clip: text;\r\n    -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;\r\n    padding: 1rem 0;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* Status Indicators */\r\n.status-success {\r\n    color: Updated README.md with Ko-fi Badge\r\nmarkdown\r\n# 🛡️ Fed-Dup\r\n\r\n### *Federated Repository Duplication Engine*\r\n\r\n[![License: MIT](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-blue.svg)](LICENSE)\r\n[![Python 3.9+](https://img.shields.io/badge/python-3.9+-blue.svg)](https://www.python.org/)\r\n[![Streamlit](https://img.shields.io/badge/Streamlit-1.28-red.svg)](https://streamlit.io/)\r\n[![Docker](https://img.shields.io/badge/Docker-Enabled-blue.svg)](https://www.docker.com/)\r\n[![CI](https://github.com/yourusername/fed-dup/actions/workflows/ci.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/yourusername/fed-dup/actions/workflows/ci.yml)\r\n[![codecov](https://codecov.io/gh/yourusername/fed-dup/branch/main/graph/badge.svg)](https://codecov.io/gh/yourusername/fed-dup)\r\n\r\n[![Ko-fi](https://ko-fi.com/img/githubbutton_sm.svg)](https://ko-fi.com/YOUR_USERNAME)\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 🎯 What is Fed-Dup?\r\n\r\n**Fed-Dup** is a lightweight, dependency-free tool that **duplicates your Git repositories** to a secondary platform. Think of it as an insurance policy for your code.\r\n\r\nIf GitHub ever goes down, your repositories are **already mirrored** on GitLab, Codeberg, or any Git server you choose.\r\n\r\n### ✨ Key Features\r\n\r\n- 🔄 **Full mirror duplication** - clones ALL branches, tags, and commit history\r\n- 🎨 **Beautiful Web UI** - manage everything through Streamlit\r\n- 🤖 **Automatic background sync** - runs hourly, no manual intervention needed\r\n- 🧹 **Disk space management** - delete local cache after sync if you want\r\n- 🐳 **Docker-ready** - deploy in one command\r\n- 🔒 **Secure** - tokens stored locally, never logged\r\n- 💾 **No database** - everything stored in a single JSON file\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 🚀 Quick Start\r\n\r\n### Option 1: Docker (Recommended)\r\n\r\n```bash\r\ngit clone https://github.com/yourusername/fed-dup\r\ncd fed-dup\r\ncp config.json.example config.json\r\n# Edit config.json with your tokens and repos\r\ndocker-compose up -d\r\nAccess the UI at http://localhost:8501\r\n\r\nOption 2: Local Installation\r\nbash\r\ngit clone https://github.com/yourusername/fed-dup\r\ncd fed-dup\r\npip install -r requirements.txt\r\ncp config.json.example config.json\r\n# Edit config.json with your tokens and repos\r\nstreamlit run app.py\r\n🔑 Setting Up Tokens\r\nGitHub Personal Access Token\r\nGo to GitHub Settings → Developer Settings → Personal Access Tokens\r\n\r\nGenerate a token with repo scope\r\n\r\nCopy the token\r\n\r\nBackup Platform Token\r\nGitLab: Settings → Access Tokens → api scope\r\n\r\nGitea: Settings → Applications → Generate token\r\n\r\nOther: Use password or SSH (SSH not yet supported)\r\n\r\n📁 Configuration\r\nFed-Dup uses a single config.json file:\r\n\r\njson\r\n{\r\n  "github_token": "ghp_xxxxx",\r\n  "backup_token": "glpat_xxxxx",\r\n  "settings": {\r\n    "auto_sync_interval": 3600,\r\n    "cleanup_after_sync": false,\r\n    "max_parallel_syncs": 3\r\n  },\r\n  "repositories": [\r\n    {\r\n      "name": "my-project",\r\n      "source": "https://github.com/username/my-project.git",\r\n      "destination": "https://gitlab.com/username/my-project.git"\r\n    }\r\n  ]\r\n}\r\n🏗️ Architecture\r\ntext\r\n┌─────────────┐      ┌──────────────┐      ┌─────────────────┐\r\n│  GitHub     │ ──▶ │  Fed-Dup     │ ──▶ │  Backup Host   │\r\n│  (Source)   │      │  Engine      │      │  (GitLab/etc)  │\r\n└─────────────┘      └──────────────┘      └─────────────────┘\r\n                            │\r\n                            ▼\r\n                     ┌──────────────┐\r\n                     │  Web UI      │\r\n                     │  (Streamlit) │\r\n                     └──────────────┘\r\n🛠️ Development\r\nFile Structure\r\ntext\r\nfed-dup/\r\n├── app.py              # Streamlit UI\r\n├── worker.py           # Auto-sync background worker\r\n├── feddup/\r\n│   ├── __init__.py\r\n│   ├── engine.py       # Core duplication logic\r\n│   ├── config.py       # Config management\r\n│   ├── utils.py        # Validation & cleanup\r\n│   └── logger.py       # Logging setup\r\n├── Dockerfile\r\n├── docker-compose.yml\r\n└── config.json.example\r\nRunning Tests\r\nbash\r\npython -m unittest discover tests/\r\n🔐 Security Notes\r\nTokens are stored only in config.json on your local machine/server\r\n\r\nTokens are never displayed in logs or UI\r\n\r\nAlways use .gitignore to prevent committing config.json\r\n\r\nUse read-only tokens when possible\r\n\r\n🤝 Contributing\r\nFork the repo\r\n\r\nCreate a feature branch\r\n\r\nCommit your changes\r\n\r\nOpen a Pull Request\r\n\r\nSee CONTRIBUTING.md for more details.\r\n\r\n📄 License\r\nMIT License - see LICENSE file\r\n\r\n💬 Support\r\nIssues: GitHub Issues\r\n\r\nDiscussions: GitHub
